importlib.import_module(module_name)\\n    return module.apply_rotary_pos_emb\\n\\n\\ndef patch_attention(model, allocations):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Monkey-patch attention layers to apply per-head compression.\\n\\n    Returns dict of original forward methods for restoration.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    config = model.config\\n    num_kv_heads = config.num_key_value_heads\\n    num_q_heads = config.num_attention_heads\\n    head_dim = config.hidden_size // num_q_heads\\n    n_groups = num_q_heads // num_kv_heads\\n\\n    rotary_fn = _get_rotary_fn(model)\\n    original_forwards = {}\\n\\n    for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\n        attn = layer.self_attn\\n\\n        head_allocs = {}\\n        for h in range(num_kv_heads):\\n            key = f\\\"{layer_idx},{h}\\\"\\n            if key in allocations:\\n                atype, param = allocations[key]\\n                head_allocs[h] = (atype, param)\\n            else:\\n                head_allocs[h] = (\\\"full\\\", 16)\\n\\n        all_full = all(a[0] == \\\"full\\\" for a in head_allocs.values())\\n        if all_full:\\n            continue\\n\\n        has_eviction = any(a[0] == \\\"evict\\\" for a in head_allocs.values())\\n        original_forwards[layer_idx] = attn.forward\\n\\n        def make_forward(orig_attn, h_allocs, has_evict, n_kv=num_kv_heads, ng=n_groups, hd=head_dim, rope_fn=rotary_fn):\\n            def compressed_forward(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):\\n                bsz, seq_len = hidden_states.shape[:2]\\n\\n                q = orig_attn.q_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, -1, hd).transpose(1, 2)\\n                k = orig_attn.k_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, n_kv, hd).transpose(1, 2)\\n                v = orig_attn.v_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, n_kv, hd).transpose(1, 2)\\n\\n                cos, sin = position_embeddings\\n                q, k = rope_fn(q, k, cos, sin)\\n\\n                # Per-head quantization\\n                for h in range(n_kv):\\n                    atype, param = h_allocs[h]\\n                    if atype == \\\"quant\\\" and int(param) < 16:\\n                        bits = int(param)\\n                        k[:, h] = quantize_per_channel(k[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)\\n                        v[:, h] = quantize_per_token(v[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)\\n\\n                k_exp = repeat_kv(k, ng)\\n                v_exp = repeat_kv(v, ng)\\n\\n                attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, k_exp.transpose(2, 3)) * orig_attn.scaling\\n                if attention_mask is not None:\\n                    cm = attention_mask[:, :, :, :k_exp.shape[-2]]\\n                    attn_scores = attn_scores + cm\\n                else:\\n                    causal = torch.triu(\\n                        torch.full((seq_len, seq_len), float(\\\"-inf\\\"),\\n                                   device=attn_scores.device, dtype=attn_scores.dtype),\\n                        diagonal=1\\n                    )\\n                    attn_scores = attn_scores + causal\\n\\n                # Eviction: compute importance from initial softmax, then mask and re-softmax\\n                if has_evict:\\n                    soft_w = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32)\\n                    for h in range(n_kv):\\n                        atype, param = h_allocs[h]\\n                        if atype == \\\"evict\\\":\\n                            ratio = float(param)\\n                            skv = k.shape[2]\\n                            n_keep = max(1, int(skv * ratio))\\n                            qs, qe = h * ng, (h + 1) * ng\\n                            importance = soft_w[:, qs:qe].mean(dim=(0, 1, 2))\\n                            _, top_idx = importance.topk(n_keep)\\n                            emask = torch.full(\\n                                (skv,), float(\\\"-inf\\\"), device=k.device, dtype=attn_scores.dtype\\n                            )\\n                            emask[top_idx] = 0\\n                            attn_scores[:, qs:qe] = attn_scores[:, qs:qe] + emask\\n\\n                attn_w = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(q.dtype)\\n                out = torch.matmul(attn_w, v_exp)\\n                out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().reshape(bsz, seq_len, -1)\\n                out = orig_attn.o_proj(out)\\n                return out, attn_w\\n\\n            return compressed_forward\\n\\n        attn.forward = make_forward(attn, head_allocs, has_eviction)\\n\\n    return original_forwards\\n\\n\\ndef restore_attention(model, original_forwards):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Restore original attention forwards.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for layer_idx, fwd in original_forwards.items():\\n        model.model.layers[layer_idx].self_attn.forward = fwd\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device, stride=512):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluate perplexity on WikiText-2.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from datasets import load_dataset, Dataset\\n\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"wikitext\\\", \\\"wikitext-2-raw-v1\\\", split=\\\"test\\\")\\n    except Exception:\\n        arrow_path = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets/wikitext/wikitext-2-raw-v1/0.0.0/b08601e04326c79dfdd32d625aee71d232d685c3/wikitext-test.arrow\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"  Hub unavailable, loading from arrow: {arrow_path}\\\")\\n        ds = Dataset.from_file(arrow_path)\\n    text = \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\".join([t for t in ds[\\\"text\\\"] if t.strip()])\\n\\n    encodings = tokenizer(text, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")\\n    input_ids = encodings.input_ids.to(device)\\n    total_len = input_ids.shape[1]\\n\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\n    total_tokens = 0\\n    n_chunks = (total_len - 1) // stride\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Evaluating perplexity on {total_len} tokens, stride={stride}, {n_chunks} chunks\\\")\\n\\n    for i, begin in enumerate(range(0, total_len - 1, stride)):\\n        end = min(begin + stride, total_len)\\n        chunk = input_ids[:, begin:end]\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = model(chunk, use_cache=False, cache_position=torch.arange(chunk.shape[1], device=chunk.device))\\n            logits = outputs.logits\\n\\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1].contiguous()\\n        shift_labels = chunk[:, 1:].contiguous()\\n\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),\\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\\n            reduction=\\\"sum\\\",\\n        )\\n        total_loss += loss.item()\\n        total_tokens += shift_labels.numel()\\n\\n        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:\\n            print(f\\\"  chunk {i + 1}/{n_chunks}, running ppl={math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens):.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    ppl = math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens)\\n    return ppl\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model\\\", type=str, default=\\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--allocation\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--device\\\", AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\"\\n    )\\n    model = model.to(args.device)\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    print(\\\"Patching attention layers...\\\")\\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, allocations)\\n\\n    ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, args.device, args.stride)\\n    print(f\\\"Perplexity: {ppl:.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\\n    return ppl\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, config.num_attention_heads\\n    head_dim = config.hidden_size // num_q_heads\\n    n_groups = num_q_heads // num_kv_heads\\n\\n    rotary_fn = _get_rotary_fn(model)\\n    original_forwards = {}\\n\\n    for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\n        attn = layer.self_attn\\n\\n        head_allocs = {}\\n        for h in range(num_kv_heads):\\n            key = f\\\"{layer_idx},{h}\\\"\\n            if key in allocations:\\n                atype, param = allocations[key]\\n                head_allocs[h] = (atype, param)\\n            else:\\n                head_allocs[h] = (\\\"full\\\", 16)\\n\\n        all_full = all(a[0] == \\\"full\\\" for a in head_allocs.values())\\n        if all_full:\\n            continue\\n\\n        has_eviction = any(a[0] == \\\"evict\\\" for a in head_allocs.values())\\n        original_forwards[layer_idx] = attn.forward\\n\\n        def make_forward(orig_attn, h_allocs, has_evict, n_kv=num_kv_heads, ng=n_groups, hd=head_dim, rope_fn=rotary_fn):\\n            def compressed_forward(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):\\n                bsz, seq_len = hidden_states.shape[:2]\\n\\n                q = orig_attn.q_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, -1, hd).transpose(1, 2)\\n                k = orig_attn.k_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, n_kv, hd).transpose(1, 2)\\n                v = orig_attn.v_proj(hidden_states).view(bsz, seq_len, n_kv, hd).transpose(1, 2)\\n\\n                cos, sin = position_embeddings\\n                q, k = rope_fn(q, k, cos, sin)\\n\\n                # Per-head quantization\\n                for h in range(n_kv):\\n                    atype, param = h_allocs[h]\\n                    if atype == \\\"quant\\\" and int(param) < 16:\\n                        bits = int(param)\\n                        k[:, h] = quantize_per_channel(k[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)\\n                        v[:, h] = quantize_per_token(v[:, h:h + 1], bits).squeeze(1)\\n\\n                k_exp = repeat_kv(k, ng)\\n                v_exp = repeat_kv(v, ng)\\n\\n                attn_scores = torch.matmul(q, k_exp.transpose(2, 3)) * orig_attn.scaling\\n                if attention_mask is not None:\\n                    cm = attention_mask[:, :, :, :k_exp.shape[-2]]\\n                    attn_scores = attn_scores + cm\\n                else:\\n                    causal = torch.triu(\\n                        torch.full((seq_len, seq_len), float(\\\"-inf\\\"),\\n                                   device=attn_scores.device, dtype=attn_scores.dtype),\\n                        diagonal=1\\n                    )\\n                    attn_scores = attn_scores + causal\\n\\n                # Eviction: compute importance from initial softmax, then mask and re-softmax\\n                if has_evict:\\n                    soft_w = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32)\\n                    for h in range(n_kv):\\n                        atype, param = h_allocs[h]\\n                        if atype == \\\"evict\\\":\\n                            ratio = float(param)\\n                            skv = k.shape[2]\\n                            n_keep = max(1, int(skv * ratio))\\n                            qs, qe = h * ng, (h + 1) * ng\\n                            importance = soft_w[:, qs:qe].mean(dim=(0, 1, 2))\\n                            _, top_idx = importance.topk(n_keep)\\n                            emask = torch.full(\\n                                (skv,), float(\\\"-inf\\\"), device=k.device, dtype=attn_scores.dtype\\n                            )\\n                            emask[top_idx] = 0\\n                            attn_scores[:, qs:qe] = attn_scores[:, qs:qe] + emask\\n\\n                attn_w = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(q.dtype)\\n                out = torch.matmul(attn_w, v_exp)\\n                out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().reshape(bsz, seq_len, -1)\\n                out = orig_attn.o_proj(out)\\n                return out, attn_w\\n\\n            return compressed_forward\\n\\n        attn.forward = make_forward(attn, head_allocs, has_eviction)\\n\\n    return original_forwards\\n\\n\\ndef restore_attention(model, original_forwards):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Restore original attention forwards.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for layer_idx, fwd in original_forwards.items():\\n        model.model.layers[layer_idx].self_attn.forward = fwd\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, device, stride=512):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluate perplexity on WikiText-2.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from datasets import load_dataset, Dataset\\n\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"wikitext\\\", \\\"wikitext-2-raw-v1\\\", split=\\\"test\\\")\\n    except Exception:\\n        arrow_path = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/datasets/wikitext/wikitext-2-raw-v1/0.0.0/b08601e04326c79dfdd32d625aee71d232d685c3/wikitext-test.arrow\\\"\\n        print(f\\\"  Hub unavailable, loading from arrow: {arrow_path}\\\")\\n        ds = Dataset.from_file(arrow_path)\\n    text = \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\".join([t for t in ds[\\\"text\\\"] if t.strip()])\\n\\n    encodings = tokenizer(text, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\")\\n    input_ids = encodings.input_ids.to(device)\\n    total_len = input_ids.shape[1]\\n\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\n    total_tokens = 0\\n    n_chunks = (total_len - 1) // stride\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Evaluating perplexity on {total_len} tokens, stride={stride}, {n_chunks} chunks\\\")\\n\\n    for i, begin in enumerate(range(0, total_len - 1, stride)):\\n        end = min(begin + stride, total_len)\\n        chunk = input_ids[:, begin:end]\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            outputs = model(chunk, use_cache=False, cache_position=torch.arange(chunk.shape[1], device=chunk.device))\\n            logits = outputs.logits\\n\\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1].contiguous()\\n        shift_labels = chunk[:, 1:].contiguous()\\n\\n        loss = F.cross_entropy(\\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.shape[-1]),\\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\\n            reduction=\\\"sum\\\",\\n        )\\n        total_loss += loss.item()\\n        total_tokens += shift_labels.numel()\\n\\n        if (i + 1) % 50 == 0:\\n            print(f\\\"  chunk {i + 1}/{n_chunks}, running ppl={math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens):.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    ppl = math.exp(total_loss / total_tokens)\\n    return ppl\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model\\\", type=str, default=\\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--allocation\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--device\\\", type=str, default=\\\"cuda:0\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--stride\\\", type=int, default=512)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    with open(args.allocation) as f:\\n        alloc_data = json.load(f)\\n    allocations = alloc_data[\\\"allocation\\\"]\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading model {args.model}...\\\")\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\"\\n    )\\n    model = model.to(args.device)\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    print(\\\"Patching attention layers...\\\")\\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, allocations)\\n\\n    ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, args.device, args.stride)\\n    print(f\\\"Perplexity: {ppl:.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\\n    return ppl\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()\n    print(f\"eval_ppl.py MD5: {eval_md5}\", flush=True)\n\n    print(f\"Loading model {MODEL_PATH}...\", flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation=\"eager\"\n    )\n    model = model.to(DEVICE)\n    model.eval()\n\n    results = {\n        \"eval_script_md5\": eval_md5,\n        \"eval_params\": {\"stride\": STRIDE, \"dataset\": \"wikitext-2-raw-v1\", \"dtype\": Uniform 4-bit\n    print(\"\\n=== Uniform 4-bit ===\", flush=True)\n    t0 = time.time()\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, UNIFORM_4BIT)\n    uniform_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE, STRIDE)\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n    results[\"uniform_4bit_bf16_ppl\"] = uniform_ppl\n    print(f\"Uniform 4-bit PPL: {uniform_ppl:.4f} ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    # 3. Hires MCKP\n    print(\"\\n=== Hires MCKP ===\", flush=True)\n    t0 = time.time()\n    orig_fwds = patch_attention(model, HIRES_ALLOCATION)\n    hires_ppl = evaluate_perplexity(model, tokenizer, DEVICE, STRIDE)\n    restore_attention(model, orig_fwds)\n    results[\"hires_mckp_bf16_ppl\"] = hires_ppl\n    results[\"bit_allocation\"] = HIRES_ALLOCATION\n    print(f\"Hires MCKP PPL: {hires_ppl:.4f} ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n=== Summary ===\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Baseline:     {baseline_ppl:.4f}\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Uniform 4b:   {uniform_ppl:.4f} (delta: +{uniform_ppl - baseline_ppl:.4f})\", flush=True)\n    print(f\"Hires MCKP:   {hires_ppl:.4f} (delta: +{hires_ppl - baseline_ppl:.4f})\", flush=True)\n\n    with open(\"/root/ratekv/hires_reeval_results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Results saved to /root/ratekv/hires_reeval_results.json\", flush=True)\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF\necho \"Script written OK\"",

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
OK, it's running now. The running PPL ~14.89-15.08 was from the UNIFORM 4-bit evaluation (the log I saw earlier was from a previous run that overlapped in the log file since I truncated). Wait, looking more carefully:

The latest tail shows:
- chunk 250/583 ppl=14.89
- chunk 300/583 ppl=15.08  
- chunk 350/583 ppl=14.96
- [null bytes - from previous log data being overwritten]
- Then HF retry messages


- Then "Using the latest cached version of the dataset..."
- Then "Evaluating perplexity on 298938 tokens, stride=512, 583 chunks"
- chunk 50/583 ppl=8.92
- chunk 100/583 ppl=9.72

This is confusing because the PPL values around 14.89 should be from the uniform 4-bit step, but that's step 2 in my script while baseline is step 1. The log file is clearly mixing data from my previous run with the new one since I'm overwriting it with `>` instead of appending.

Looking at the latest chunks though—50 with ppl=8.92 and 100 with ppl=9.72—these match the baseline numbers, so the v2 script is actually running correctly despite the null bytes and file truncation artifacts. I should just wait for the baseline evaluation to finish and check back in a few minutes.
```

Hires reeval 完成。Hard blocker 已解除。

## 最终结果（当前 eval_ppl.py, MD5: 26d218ac）

| 配置 | PPL | vs Baseline | vs 旧数字 |
|------|-----|-------------|-----------|
| **Baseline bf16** | 9.8106 | — | 旧 8.819 (+0.99) |
| **Uniform 4-bit bf16** | 15.4454 | +5.6348 | 旧 fp16 15.45 (一致) |
| **Hires MCKP bf16** | 10.9131 | +1.1025 | 旧 9.569 (+1.34) |

关键发现：
- **旧 eval 确实低估了 ~1.0 PPL**，baseline 从 8.819→9.8106，hires 从 9.569→10.9131
- Hires MCKP 相对 uniform 4-bit 的降级减少了 **80.4%** (1.10/5.63)
- Uniform 4-bit bf16 与 fp16 的 PPL 几乎无差异（15.4454 vs 15.45）
- 结果文件已保存到服务器 `/root/ratekv/hires_reeval_results.json`
